# Package net.thevpc.nuts.core

<a id="pkg-net-thevpc-nuts-core"></a>

Nuts : Network Updatable Things Service (universal package manager) is
a new Open Source Package Manager to help install packages
and libraries for runtime execution.


**nuts** stands for **Network Updatable Things Services** tool.
It's a **Package Manager** for (mainly) Java
and non Java world. It helps discovering, downloading, assembling and executing  remote artifacts (packages) in
a very handy way.




Each managed artifact  is also called a **nuts** which  is a __Network Updatable Thing Service__ .
**nuts** artifacts are  stored  into repositories. A  repository  may be local for  storing
local **nuts** or remote for accessing  remote artifacts (good examples  are  remote maven
repositories). It may also be a proxy repository so that remote artifacts are fetched and cached locally to save network
resources.




One manages a set of repositories called a  workspace. Managed **nuts**  (artifacts)  have descriptors
that depict dependencies between them. This dependency is seamlessly handled by  **nuts**  (tool) to
resolve and download on-need dependencies over the wire.




**nuts** is a swiss army knife tool as it acts like (and supports) maven build tool to have an abstract
view of the  artifacts dependency and like  __zypper__/__apt-get__/__pip__/__npm__  package manager tools
to install and uninstall artifacts allowing multiple versions of the very same artifact to  be installed.





Although **nuts** focuses on java artifacts, it still supports, by design, native and all non java artifacts.
Dependencies are fetched according to the current operating system type, distribution and hardware
architectures.





**nuts** works either as standalone application or as java library to enable dynamic and dependency
aware class loading and brings a rich toolset to provide a versatile portable command line tools such
as **nsh** (a bash like shell), **tomcat**, **derby** ... wrapper tools to make it easier providing
development , test and deployment reproducible environments.





With container concepts in mind, **nuts** is the perfect java application tool for __Docker__, __CoreOs__
and other container engines.




**COMMON VERBS:**


- install,uninstall,update : install/uninstall/update an artifact (using its fetched/deployed installer)
- deploy, undeploy         : manage artifacts on the local repositories
- fetch,push               : download, upload to remote repositories
- search                   : search for existing/installable artifacts
- exec                     : execute an artifact (tool)

```java
NBootOptionsBuilder reset(Boolean reset)
```

set the 'reset' flag to delete the workspace folders and files.
This is equivalent to the following nuts command line options :
"-Z" and "--reset" options

**Parameters:**

- `reset` - reset flag, when null inherit default ('false')

**Returns:**

`this` instance



---

<a id="m-NBootOptionsBuilder-resetHard"></a>

#### resetHard

```java
NBootOptionsBuilder resetHard(Boolean resetHard)
```

set resetHard flag.
Reset hard is used at boot time to reset ALL of nuts workspaces

**Parameters:**

- `resetHard` - resetHard flag

**Returns:**

`this`

<a id="m-NBootOptionsBuilder-skipBoot"></a>

#### skipBoot

```java
NBootOptionsBuilder skipBoot(Boolean skipBoot)
```

if true, do not bootstrap workspace after reset/recover.
When reset/recover is not active this option is not accepted and an error will be thrown


defaults to false.


**option-type :** runtime (available only for the current workspace instance)

**Parameters:**

- `skipBoot` - skipBoot

**Returns:**

if true, do not run welcome when no application arguments were resolved

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-core-NConfirmationMode"></a>

## NConfirmationMode

```java
public enum NConfirmationMode implements NEnum
```

user interaction mode. Some operations may require user confirmation before
performing critical operations such as overriding existing values, deleting
sensitive information ; in such cases several modes are available : either
to require user interaction (ASK mode, the default value) or force the
processing (YES mode), or ignoring the processing and continuing the next
(NO) or cancel the processing and exit with an error message (ERROR)

**Author:** thevpc

**Since:** 0.5.5

### Enum Constants

| Constant | Description |
| :--- | :--- |
| `ASK` | force interactive mode |
| `YES` | non interactive mode, always perform operation |
| `NO` | non interactive mode, ignore operation and process next |
| `ERROR` | non interactive mode, throw exception |

### Fields

| Modifier and Type | Field | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `private final String` | `id` | lower-cased identifier for the enum entry |

### Constructors

```

N confirmation mode.

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-core-NIndexStore"></a>

## NIndexStore

```java
public interface NIndexStore
```

Classes implementations of `NutsIndexStore` handle
indexing of repositories to enable faster search.

**Author:** thevpc

**Since:** 0.5.4

### Method Summary

| Modifier and Type | Method | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `NIterator&lt;NId&gt;` | [searchVersions(NId)](#m-NIndexStore-searchVersions) | search all versions of the given artifact |
| `NIterator&lt;NId&gt;` | [search(NDefinitionFilter)](#m-NIndexStore-search) | search all artifacts matching the given filter |
| `boolean` | [isEnabled()](#m-NIndexStore-isEnabled) | return true if the index is enabled |
| `NIndexStore` | [enabled(boolean)](#m-NIndexStore-enabled) | enable of disable ot index |
| `NIndexStore` | [invalidate(NId)](#m-NIndexStore-invalidate) | invalidate the artifact from the index |
| `NIndexStore` | [revalidate(NId)](#m-NIndexStore-revalidate) | invalidate the artifact from the index and re-index it |
| `NIndexStore` | [subscribe()](#m-NIndexStore-subscribe) | subscribe the current repository so the indexing is processed. |
| `NIndexStore` | [unsubscribe()](#m-NIndexStore-unsubscribe) | unsubscribe the current repository so that the indexing is disabled and the index is removed. |
| `boolean` | [isSubscribed()](#m-NIndexStore-isSubscribed) | return true if the current repository is registered |

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-core-NIsolationLevel"></a>

## NIsolationLevel

```java
public enum NIsolationLevel implements NEnum
```

NIsolationLevel enum.

**Author:** thevpc

**Since:** 0.8.0

### Enum Constants

| Constant | Description |
| :--- | :--- |
| `USER` | The User Workspace Isolation Level runs a workspace in a dedicated location under the regular user's privileges. |
| `SYSTEM` | The System Workspace Isolation Level is used to share a workspace among all users on the same machine. |
| `CONFINED` | The Confined Workspace Isolation Level runs a workspace in a specific location as a regular user, without interfering with other workspaces belonging... |
| `SANDBOX` | The Sandbox Isolation Level runs the workspace in a temporary location, spawning a fresh instance each time. |
| `MEMORY` | The Memory Isolation Level runs the workspace entirely in memory, without storing its configuration on disk. |

### Fields

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-core-NNoSessionException"></a>

## NNoSessionException

```java
public class NNoSessionException extends RuntimeException implements NExceptionBase
```

Base Boot Nuts Exception. Thrown when the Workspace could is booting
and is not yet available.

**Author:** thevpc

**Since:** 0.5.4

### Fields

| Modifier and Type | Field | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `private final NMsg` | `message` |  |

### Constructors

#### `NNoSessionException()`

```java
public NNoSessionException()
```

Constructs a 'missing session' exception


#### `NNoSessionException(NMsg message)`

```java
public NNoSessionException(NMsg message)
```

Constructs a new runtime exception with the specified detail message.
The cause is not initialized, and may subsequently be initialized by a
call to [#initCause](#m-initCause).

**Parameters:**

- `message` - the detail message. The detail message is saved for
               later retrieval by the [#message()](#m-message) method.


#### `NNoSessionException(NMsg message, Throwable cause)`

```java
public NNoSessionException(NMsg message, Throwable cause)
```

Constructs a new runtime exception with the specified detail message and
cause.


Note that the detail message associated with
`cause` is __not__ automatically incorporated in
this runtime exception's detail message.

**Parameters:**

- `message` - the detail message (which is saved for later retrieval
               by the [#message()](#m-message) method).
- `cause` - the cause (which is saved for later retrieval by the
               [#getCause()](#m-getCause) method).  (A `null` value is
               permitted, and indicates that the cause is nonexistent or
               unknown.)


### Method Summary

| Modifier and Type | Method | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `NMsg` | [formattedMessage()](#m-NNoSessionException-formattedMessage) | Formatted message. |

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-core-NOpenMode"></a>

## NOpenMode

```java
public enum NOpenMode implements NEnum
```

**Author:** thevpc

**Since:** 0.5.4 (renamed from NutsWorkspaceOpenMode to NutsOpenMode in 0.8.1)

### Enum Constants

| Constant | Description |
| :--- | :--- |
| `OPEN_OR_CREATE` | Open or Create. |
| `CREATE_OR_ERROR` | Create Workspace (if not found) or throw Error (if found) if not exists, then create and open if exists, then error |
| `OPEN_OR_ERROR` | Open Workspace (if found) or throw Error (if not found) if not exists, then error if exists, then open |
| `OPEN_OR_NULL` | Open Workspace (if found) or return null if not exists, then open if exists, then return null |

| :--- | :--- | :--- |

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-core-NRepositoryAlreadyRegisteredException"></a>

## NRepositoryAlreadyRegisteredException

```java
public class NRepositoryAlreadyRegisteredException extends NRepositoryException
```

This exception is thrown when a repository location could no be loaded because
the repository is already registered for the actual workspace.

**Since:** 0.5.4

### Constructors

#### `NRepositoryAlreadyRegisteredException(String repository)`

```java
public NRepositoryAlreadyRegisteredException(String repository)
```

Constructs a new NutsNotInstalledException exception

**Parameters:**

- `repository` - repository


#### `NRepositoryAlreadyRegisteredException(String repository, Throwable err)`

```java
public NRepositoryAlreadyRegisteredException(String repository, Throwable err)
```

Constructs a new NutsNotInstalledException exception

**Parameters:**

- `repository` - repository
- `err` - error



---

<a id="m-NSession-isPlainTrace"></a>

#### isPlainTrace

```java
boolean isPlainTrace()
```

true if non iterable and plain formats along with trace flag are armed.
equivalent to ```java
isTrace()
&& !isIterableOut()
&& getOutputFormat() == NutsContentType.PLAIN
```

**Returns:**

true plain non iterable format AND trace are armed



---

<a id="m-NSession-isIterableTrace"></a>

#### isIterableTrace

```java
boolean isIterableTrace()
```

true if iterable format and trace flag are armed. equivalent to ```java
isTrace()
&& isIterableOut()
```

**Returns:**

true plain non iterable format AND trace are armed



---

boolean isStructuredTrace()
```

true if NON iterable and NON plain formats along with trace flag are
armed. equivalent to ```java
isTrace()
&& !isIterableOut()
&& getOutputFormat() == NutsContentType.PLAIN
```

**Returns:**

true if NON iterable and NON plain formats along with trace flag
are armed.

<a id="m-NSession-setProperty"></a>

#### setProperty

```java
NSession setProperty(String property, Object value)
```

Sets a property in this session.



By default, this is a session-local property that is dropped when the session is copied.
Passing `null` is equivalent to removing the property.
Equivalent to [Object)](#m-setSessionProperty).

**Parameters:**

- `property` - the name of the property
- `value` - the value to store, or `null` to remove

**Returns:**

this `NSession` instance, for fluent usage



---

<a id="m-NSession-setProperty"></a>

#### setProperty

```java
<T> NSession setProperty(Class<T> property, T value)
```

Sets a property in this session.



By default, this is a session-local property that is dropped when the session is copied.
Passing `null` is equivalent to removing the property.
Equivalent to [Object)](#m-setSessionProperty).

**Parameters:**

- `property` - the name of the property
- `value` - the value to store, or `null` to remove

**Returns:**

this `NSession` instance, for fluent usage



---

<a id="m-NSession-setSessionProperty"></a>

#### setSessionProperty

```java
NSession setSessionProperty(String property, Object value)
```

Sets a session-local property that is not copied when the session is duplicated.



By default, this is a session-local property that is dropped when the session is copied.
Passing `null` is equivalent to removing the property.
Equivalent to [Object)](#m-setSessionProperty).

**Parameters:**

- `property` - the name of the property
- `value` - the value to store, or `null` to remove

**Returns:**

this `NSession` instance, for fluent usage



---

<a id="m-NSession-setTransitiveProperty"></a>

#### setTransitiveProperty

```java
NSession setTransitiveProperty(String property, Object value) throws NNonCopiableException
```

Sets a property that is copied when this session is duplicated.



The value must either be immutable (primitive, boxed, `String`, `Number`, `java.time.temporal.Temporal`,
[Enum](#cls-Enum), or a class annotated with [net.thevpc.nuts.util.NImmutable](#cls-net-thevpc-nuts-util-NImmutable)) or implement [net.thevpc.nuts.util.NCopiable](#cls-net-thevpc-nuts-util-NCopiable).



Passing `null` is equivalent to removing the property.

**Parameters:**

- `property` - the name of the property
- `value` - the value to store, or `null` to remove

**Returns:**

this `NSession` instance

**Throws:**

- `net.thevpc.nuts.util.NNonCopiableException` - if the computed value is not copyable or immutable



---

<a id="m-NSession-setTransitiveProperty"></a>

#### setTransitiveProperty

```java
<T> NSession setTransitiveProperty(Class<T> property, T value) throws NNonCopiableException
```

Sets a property that is copied when this session is duplicated.



The value must either be immutable (primitive, boxed, `String`, `Number`, `java.time.temporal.Temporal`,
[Enum](#cls-Enum), or a class annotated with [net.thevpc.nuts.util.NImmutable](#cls-net-thevpc-nuts-util-NImmutable)) or implement [net.thevpc.nuts.util.NCopiable](#cls-net-thevpc-nuts-util-NCopiable).



Passing `null` is equivalent to removing the property.

**Parameters:**

- `property` - the name of the property
- `value` - the value to store, or `null` to remove

**Returns:**

this `NSession` instance

**Throws:**

- `net.thevpc.nuts.util.NNonCopiableException` - if the computed value is not copyable or immutable



---

<a id="m-NSession-setSharedProperty"></a>

#### setSharedProperty

```java
NSession setSharedProperty(String property, Object value)
```

Sets a property that is shared by reference with all descendant sessions.



Changes to this value in one session are visible in all copies.



Passing `null` is equivalent to removing the property.

**Parameters:**

- `property` - the name of the property
- `value` - the value to store, or `null` to remove

**Returns:**

this `NSession` instance



---

<a id="m-NSession-setSharedProperty"></a>

#### setSharedProperty

```java
<T> NSession setSharedProperty(Class<T> property, T value)
```

Sets a property that is shared by reference with all descendant sessions.



Changes to this value in one session are visible in all copies.



Passing `null` is equivalent to removing the property.

**Parameters:**

- `property` - the name of the property
- `value` - the value to store, or `null` to remove

**Returns:**

this `NSession` instance



---

```java
Map<String,Object> properties()
```

Returns a read-only view of all properties in this session.

**Returns:**

a map of property names to their current values



---

<a id="m-NSession-getProperty"></a>

#### getProperty

```java
NOptional<Object> getProperty(String property)
```

Retrieves the current value of a property by name.



This does not expose any copy or scope semantics; it returns the value
currently visible in this session.

**Parameters:**

- `property` - the name of the property

**Returns:**

an [NOptional](#cls-NOptional) containing the property value if present, or empty otherwise



---

<a id="m-NSession-getProperty"></a>

#### getProperty

```java
<T> NOptional<T> getProperty(Class<T> propertyTypeAndName)
```

Retrieves the current value of a property identified by type.



Useful for type-safe property storage.

**Parameters:**

- `propertyTypeAndName` - the class of the property

**Returns:**

an [NOptional](#cls-NOptional) containing the property value if present, or empty otherwise



---

<a id="m-NSession-getOrComputeProperty"></a>

#### getOrComputeProperty

```java
<T> T getOrComputeProperty(Class<T> property, Supplier<T> supplier)
```

Retrieves an existing property of the given type, or computes and stores it
using the supplied function if absent.

**Parameters:**

- `property` - the class identifying the property
- `supplier` - the function to compute the property if absent

**Returns:**

the existing or newly computed property



---

<a id="m-NSession-getOrComputeProperty"></a>

#### getOrComputeProperty

```java
<T> T getOrComputeProperty(String property, Supplier<T> supplier)
```

Retrieves an existing property by name, or computes and stores it
using the supplied function if absent.

**Parameters:**

- `property` - the name of the property
- `supplier` - the function to compute the property if absent

**Returns:**

the existing or newly computed property



---

<a id="m-NSession-getOrComputeSessionProperty"></a>

#### getOrComputeSessionProperty

```java
<T> T getOrComputeSessionProperty(String property, Supplier<T> supplier)
```

Retrieves the value of a session-local property by name, or computes it if absent.



The property is stored in the current session only. It will **not** be
copied to descendant sessions if this session is copied.

**Parameters:**

- `property` - the property name
- `supplier` - the supplier to compute the value if the property is absent

**Returns:**

the existing or newly computed property value

**Throws:**

- `NAssertException` - if `supplier` is null



---

<a id="m-NSession-getOrComputeSharedProperty"></a>

#### getOrComputeSharedProperty

```java
<T> T getOrComputeSharedProperty(String property, Supplier<T> supplier)
```

Retrieves the value of a shared property by name, or computes it if absent.



The property is shared by reference across this session and all descendant sessions
created via [NSession#copy()](#cls-NSession). Mutating the value affects all sessions.

**Parameters:**

- `property` - the property name
- `supplier` - the supplier to compute the value if the property is absent

**Returns:**

the existing or newly computed property value

**Throws:**

- `NAssertException` - if `supplier` is null



---

<a id="m-NSession-getOrComputeTransitiveProperty"></a>

#### getOrComputeTransitiveProperty

```java
<T> T getOrComputeTransitiveProperty(String property, Supplier<T> supplier)
```

Retrieves the value of a transitive property by name, or computes it if absent.



The property is copied when this session is copied:


- If the value is immutable, it is reused directly.
- If the value implements [net.thevpc.nuts.util.NCopiable](#cls-net-thevpc-nuts-util-NCopiable), `copy()` is called to create
        a new instance for the descendant session.
- Otherwise, an exception is thrown.

**Parameters:**

- `property` - the property name
- `supplier` - the supplier to compute the value if the property is absent

**Returns:**

the existing or newly computed property value

**Throws:**

- `NullPointerException` - if `supplier` is null
- `NAssertException` - if `supplier` is null



---

<a id="m-NSession-getOrComputeSessionProperty"></a>

#### getOrComputeSessionProperty

```java
<T> T getOrComputeSessionProperty(Class<T> property, Supplier<T> supplier)
```

Retrieves the value of a session-local property for a class-based key, or computes it if absent.



Equivalent to [Supplier)](#m-getOrComputeSessionProperty), using
`property.getName()` as the key.

**Parameters:**

- `property` - the class used as property key
- `supplier` - the supplier to compute the value if absent

**Returns:**

the existing or newly computed property value

**Throws:**

- `NAssertException` - if `supplier` is null



---

<a id="m-NSession-getOrComputeSharedProperty"></a>

#### getOrComputeSharedProperty

```java
<T> T getOrComputeSharedProperty(Class<T> property, Supplier<T> supplier)
```

Retrieves the value of a shared property for a class-based key, or computes it if absent.



Equivalent to [Supplier)](#m-getOrComputeSharedProperty), using
`property.getName()` as the key.

**Parameters:**

- `property` - the class used as property key
- `supplier` - the supplier to compute the value if absent

**Returns:**

the existing or newly computed property value

**Throws:**

- `NAssertException` - if `supplier` is null



---

<a id="m-NSession-getOrComputeTransitiveProperty"></a>

#### getOrComputeTransitiveProperty

```java
<T> T getOrComputeTransitiveProperty(Class<T> property, Supplier<T> supplier) throws NNonCopiableException
```

Retrieves the value of a transitive property for a class-based key, or computes it if absent.



Equivalent to [Supplier)](#m-getOrComputeTransitiveProperty), using
`property.getName()` as the key.

**Parameters:**

- `property` - the class used as property key
- `supplier` - the supplier to compute the value if absent

**Returns:**

the existing or newly computed property value

**Throws:**

- `NAssertException` - if `supplier` is null
- `net.thevpc.nuts.util.NNonCopiableException` - if the computed value is not copyable or immutable



---

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-core-NSessionAware"></a>

## NSessionAware

```java
public interface NSessionAware
```

classes that implement this class will have their method [#session(NSession)](#m-session)
called upon its creation (by factory) with a non `null` argument to **initialize**.
They **may** accept a call with a `null`
argument later to **dispose** the instance.

**Author:** thevpc

### Method Summary

| Modifier and Type | Method | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `void` | [session(NSession)](#m-NSessionAware-session) | initialize or dispose the instance. |

### Method Details


---

<a id="m-NSessionAware-session"></a>

#### session

```java
void session(NSession session)
```

initialize or dispose the instance.
when session is not null, the instance should initialize it values
accordingly.
when session is null, the instance should dispose resources.

**Parameters:**

- `session` - session reference or null



---

<a id="m-NWorkspace-addRepository"></a>

#### addRepository

```java
NRepository addRepository(String repositoryNamedUrl)
```

creates a new repository from the given
`repositoryNamedUrl`.Accepted `repositoryNamedUrl` values are
:


- 'local' : corresponds to a local updatable repository.



will be named 'local'


- 'm2', '.m2', 'maven-local' : corresponds the local maven folder
repository. will be named 'local'

- 'maven-central': corresponds the remote maven central repository.
will be named 'local'

- 'maven-git', 'vpc-public-maven': corresponds the remote maven
vpc-public-maven git folder repository. will be named 'local'

- 'maven-git', 'nuts-public': corresponds the remote nuts
nuts-public git folder repository. will be named 'local'

- name=uri-or-path : corresponds the given uri. will be named name.
Here are some examples:


- myremote=http://192.168.6.3/folder
- myremote=/folder/subfolder
- myremote=c:/folder/subfolder

- uri-or-path : corresponds the given uri. will be named uri's last
path package name. Here are some examples:


- http://192.168.6.3/folder : will be named 'folder'
- myremote=/folder/subfolder : will be named 'folder'
- myremote=c:/folder/subfolder : will be named 'folder'

**Parameters:**

- `repositoryNamedUrl` - repositoryNamedUrl

**Returns:**

created repository



---

<a id="m-NWorkspace-properties"></a>

#### properties

```java
Map<String,Object> properties()
```

Returns a map of all properties currently defined in this workspace.



The returned map is a snapshot of the workspace properties and may be
unmodifiable depending on implementation. Use [#getProperty(String)](#m-getProperty)
or [Object)](#m-setProperty) to interact with individual properties.

**Returns:**

a map of property names to values



---

<a id="m-NWorkspace-getProperty"></a>

#### getProperty

```java
NOptional<Object> getProperty(String property)
```

Retrieves the raw value of a property by name from the workspace.



The returned value is not type-checked; use [#getProperty(Class)](#m-getProperty) for
typed access.

**Parameters:**

- `property` - the name of the property

**Returns:**

the property value wrapped in [NOptional](#cls-NOptional), empty if not present



---

<a id="m-NWorkspace-getProperty"></a>

#### getProperty

```java
<T> NOptional<T> getProperty(Class<T> propertyTypeAndName)
```

Retrieves the value of a property using a class as key.



The property key is the fully qualified class name of `propertyTypeAndName`.

**Parameters:**

- `propertyTypeAndName` - the class used as property key

**Returns:**

the property value wrapped in [NOptional](#cls-NOptional), empty if not present



---

<a id="m-NWorkspace-setProperty"></a>

#### setProperty

```java
NWorkspace setProperty(String property, Object value)
```

Sets a property value in this workspace.



If the property already exists, its value is replaced. This method returns
the workspace itself to allow fluent chaining.

**Parameters:**

- `property` - the property name
- `value` - the property value

**Returns:**

this `NWorkspace` instance



---

<a id="m-NWorkspace-getOrComputeProperty"></a>

#### getOrComputeProperty

```java
<T> T getOrComputeProperty(Class<T> property, Supplier<T> supplier)
```

Retrieves an existing property by class key, or computes it using the provided supplier if absent.



The property is added to the workspace if computed. The key is the fully qualified
name of `property`.

**Parameters:**

- `property` - the class used as property key
- `supplier` - the function to compute the property if absent

**Returns:**

the existing or newly computed property

**Throws:**

- `NullPointerException` - if `supplier` is null



---

<a id="m-NWorkspace-getOrComputeProperty"></a>

#### getOrComputeProperty

```java
<T> T getOrComputeProperty(String property, Supplier<T> supplier)
```

Retrieves an existing property by name, or computes it using the provided supplier if absent.



The property is added to the workspace if computed.

**Parameters:**

- `property` - the property name
- `supplier` - the function to compute the property if absent

**Returns:**

the existing or newly computed property

**Throws:**

- `NullPointerException` - if `supplier` is null

| Modifier and Type | Method | Description |

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-core-NWorkspaceOptions"></a>

## NWorkspaceOptions

```java
public interface NWorkspaceOptions extends Serializable
```

NWorkspaceOptions are used to create new workspaces,
whereas NBootOptions holds effective bootstrap Workspace Options

<a id="m-NWorkspaceOptionsBuilder-skipBoot"></a>

#### skipBoot

```java
NWorkspaceOptionsBuilder skipBoot(Boolean skipBoot)
```

if true, do not bootstrap workspace after reset/recover.
When reset/recover is not active this option is not accepted and an error will be thrown


defaults to false.


**option-type :** runtime (available only for the current workspace instance)

**Parameters:**

- `skipBoot` - skipBoot

**Returns:**

if true, do not run welcome when no application arguments were resolved
